            var children = image.parentNode.childNodes;
            var element = null;


            for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
                if (children[i].className != undefined && children[i].className.includes("list-image-placeholder")) {
                    element = children[i].style.display = "flex";
                    break;
                }
            }

            image.parentNode.removeChild(image);
        }
